### [28] [Rendezvous of Mobile Deterministic Automata in Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06482)
*Bibhuti Das,Andrzej Pelc*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究两个移动智能体（同构确定性有限自动机）在图中的会合（rendezvous）问题。发现若智能体不能标记节点则不存在通用DFA；即使使用任意有限数量的静止卵石也对于树不存在通用DFA；但当每个智能体配备一个可移动卵石时，可以为树设计出RV-universal DFA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在什么条件下存在通用的确定性有限自动机（DFA），使得两个智能体无论延迟多久都能在可行实例中实现会合。核心动机是搞清楚卵石的可移动性是否是实现通用会合的关键因素。

Method: 首先定义了可行实例和RV-universal DFA的概念。通过构造性反例证明：无标记能力时线路图中不存在通用DFA；静止卵石（不论数量）下树中也无通用DFA。最后通过设计一个仅需单个可移动卵石的DFA，证明对树类实例存在RV-universal DFA。

Result: 证明了卵石的可移动性是关键特征：任何有限数量的静止卵石都无法实现树的RV-universal DFA，而单一可移动卵石即可实现对所有树实例的通用会合保证。

Conclusion: 移动卵石能力本质上增强了确定性有限自动机解决会合问题的能力，单个可移动卵石就足以在树上实现通用会合，而静止标记无论如何增加数量都难以达到这一目标。

Abstract: Two mobile agents, modeled as identical deterministic finite automata (DFA) navigating in synchronous rounds in a graph with unlabeled nodes, have to meet at some node. The well-researched task of meeting in a graph is known as rendezvous. Agents start at adversarially chosen distinct nodes in possibly different rounds. An instance of the rendezvous problem is the underlying graph, together with the initial nodes $u$ and $v$ of the agents. Such an instance is feasible, if there exists a DFA (possibly working only for this instance), such that its identical copies starting at nodes $u$ and $v$, with an arbitrary delay, accomplish rendezvous. A DFA is RV-universal for a class of instances, if it guarantees rendezvous of its copies starting at the designated nodes with arbitrary delay, for all feasible instances of this class. Our goal is to investigate the existence of RV-universal DFA.
  We start by observing that if agents cannot mark nodes in any way then there does not exist a RV-universal DFA even for the class of instances where the underlying graph is a line. Hence we allow the use of identical pebbles to mark the nodes by the agents. We consider stationary pebbles that can be dropped by agents at nodes but cannot be picked up, and movable pebbles that can be dropped by agents and later picked up. We observe that, even in the more powerful scenario of movable pebbles, if agents are equipped with any finite number of pebbles, there is no RV-universal DFA for the class of all instances. Hence we restrict attention to instances where the underlying graph is a tree. Our main contribution are two contrasting results showing that movability of pebbles is a crucial feature. We first prove that for any finite number of stationary pebbles there is no RV-universal DFA for trees, and then we design a RV-universal DFA for trees, where each agent is equipped with a single movable pebble.

</details>


### [29] [Cascade: Exploiting SLO-Aware latency budget for fair and high goodput LLM inference serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06557)
*Muhammad Adnan,Rohan Mahapatra,Prashant J. Nair,Daniel Berger,Pantea Zardoshti,Rodrigo Fonseca,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cascade是一个LLM服务系统，通过定义并持续更新每个请求的"延迟预算"（SLO与预测剩余服务时间之差），统一协调请求调度和KV-cache管理，在三个生产负载trace上相比vLLM默认调度器将goodput提升最高2.4倍，SLO违反率降低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务平台用统一的响应延迟SLO管理所有请求，但同一SLO下的请求在输入长度、生成长度、执行成本和KV-cache可复用性等方面差异可达数量级，导致紧迫程度截然不同。仅用deadline控制调度顺序的方式无法充分利用延迟余量来优化资源分配。

Method: Cascade提出per-request延迟预算（latency budget）= SLO - 预测剩余服务时间，并通过一个统一的预算维度联合优化两个层面：(1) 调度器优先服务剩余预算少的请求；(2) 内存管理器根据同一预算决定非驻留KV状态是从更深层存储恢复/预取、保留在HBM还是重算。通过将排队和数据移动开销引导到能吸收的请求上，在保证异构请求类别公平性的同时提升SLO满足率。

Result: 在三个LLM的生产trace上，相比vLLM默认的FCFS调度器，Cascade将goodput提升最高达2.4倍，SLO违反率降低40%。

Conclusion: 通过统一的延迟预算概念同时驱动调度和KV-cache分层管理，Cascade证明将延迟余量感知纳入系统决策可以显著提升LLM服务的SLO满足率和整体吞吐，为异构工作负载下的LLM服务系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: The reasoning and agentic capabilities of large language models have expanded the range of applications they support, from short interactive exchanges to long, compute-heavy requests. LLM serving platforms today define response-latency service-level objectives, even though requests within the same service can differ by orders of magnitude in input length, generation length, execution cost, and the availability of reusable KV-cache state. As a result, requests governed by the same service level objective have different urgency: after accounting for the time required to execute them, some have substantial latency headroom while others have almost none. We define this headroom---the difference between a request's service level objective and its predicted remaining service time---as its per-request latency budget.
  We present Cascade, an LLM serving system that estimates and continuously updates this budget from request characteristics, KV-cache state, and current system load. Unlike prior SLO-aware schedulers that use deadlines to govern request ordering alone, Cascade uses a single per-request budget to jointly coordinate request scheduling and KV-cache management across the memory hierarchy. Its scheduler prioritizes requests with little remaining budget, while its memory manager uses the same budget to decide whether non-resident KV state should be restored or prefetched from a deeper tier, retained in HBM, or recomputed. By directing queueing and data-movement overhead toward requests that can absorb it, Cascade improves SLO-satisfied goodput while preserving fairness across heterogeneous request classes. On production traces across three large language models, Cascade improves goodput by up to2.4x and reduces SLO violations by 40% relative to the default vLLM first-come, first-served scheduler.

</details>


### [30] [MARS: A Monte Carlo Tree Search-based Adaptive and Responsive Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06629)
*Yash Kurkure,Yihe Zhang,Zhiling Lan,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MARS是一个基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的免训练HPC调度器，通过可配置的奖励函数在每次调度周期内探索未来决策后果，相比DRL和静态启发式方法，能同时优化等待时间或资源利用率，在Argonne生产系统上分别降低64%和43%的尾部等待时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高性能计算系统的作业调度依赖静态启发式和人工管理，而深度强化学习（DRL）需要历史训练数据且优化目标在训练时固定，当调度优先级变化时需重新训练模型，缺乏灵活性和适应性。

Method: MARS采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）作为核心算法，不依赖任何历史训练数据。它通过一个轻量级离散事件模拟器在严格时间预算内前瞻性地探索调度决策的未来后果，并通过可配置的奖励函数在每次调度周期适应目标，而非将目标固化在训练模型中。

Result: 在Argonne领导计算设施的两个生产系统（4,360节点的Theta和560节点的Polaris）上，使用全年生产负载进行评估。在等待时间最小化目标下，MARS-CW相对于生产环境中的WFP启发式调度器，在Theta上降低了64%的尾部等待时间，在Polaris上降低了43%；在利用率最大化目标下，MARS-CU在维护前的48小时内恢复了利用率。

Conclusion: MARS通过奖励函数重配置可以在同一框架下针对不同优化目标（如等待时间或利用率）进行调度，且免训练特性使其无需历史数据和重新训练，相比DRL和传统启发式方法具有更强的适应性和灵活性。

Abstract: Modern High Performance Computing systems depend on static heuristics and manual administration for job scheduling and reservation management. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown promising scheduling performance but requires historical training data and fixes the optimization goal at training time, forcing operators to retrain whenever priorities shift. We introduce MARS (Monte Carlo Tree Search-based Adaptive and Responsive Scheduler), a training-free HPC scheduler whose optimization goal is configurable through a reward function rather than baked into a learned model. MARS uses a lightweight discrete-event simulator to explore the future consequences of scheduling decisions within a strict time budget, adapting to the configured reward at each scheduling cycle. We evaluate MARS on year-long production workloads from two systems at Argonne Leadership Computing Facility -- 4,360-node Theta and 560-node Polaris---under two reward functions: wait-time minimization (MARS-CW) and utilization maximization (MARS-CU). Unlike DRL and heuristics, which only react to the current queue or wait for backfill to find holes, MARS exploits look-ahead to proactively drain the system and plan around future reservations, packing the system to avoid the fragmentation and utilization drop that typically precede reservation windows. MARS-CW reduces tail wait time by 64% on Theta and 43% on Polaris over the production WFP heuristic, while MARS-CU recovers utilization in the 48 hours leading into maintenance, demonstrating that MARS can target either objective via reward reconfiguration.

</details>


### [31] [DGEMM with Ozaki Scheme I/II on FP4 Tensor Cores: A Base-13 E2M1 Limb Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06812)
*Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用FP4 Tensor Core模拟FP64矩阵乘法（DGEMM）的方法，通过Ozaki scheme I和II实现高精度矩阵乘法，并在RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell上验证，在大规模问题下性能超越现有FP8实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往高精度矩阵乘法模拟基于INT8和FP8 Tensor Core，而速度更快的FP4尚未被利用；作者希望利用FP4 Tensor Core的更高吞吐实现更快的高精度矩阵乘法模拟。

Method: 利用FP4（E2M1）值的性质——翻倍后成为整数，且该整数集以13为倍数平移可覆盖所有整数——将任意整数转换为基13的FP4 limb。此转换保证中间和在FP32累加器中无误差，从而使FP4 Tensor Core可用于Ozaki scheme I和II。同原理还可将INT8整数GEMM在FP4上逐位精确模拟。另提出kernel实现优化以提升峰值性能利用率。

Result: 在RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell上验证，性能与现有FP8 Ozaki scheme II实现相当，在大问题规模（16384^3）下实际超越FP8实现；理论上当FP4吞吐为FP8两倍时，Ozaki scheme II在FP4上性能略优。

Conclusion: FP4 Tensor Core可有效用于高精度矩阵乘法模拟，结合理论优势与kernel优化，在大规模问题上实现了对FP8方案的性能超越。

Abstract: This paper proposes a method and its implementation for emulating FP64 matrix multiplication (DGEMM) by constructing, on FP4 (E2M1; 2 exponent bits and 1 mantissa bit) Tensor Cores, Ozaki schemes I and II, which realize high-precision matrix multiplication on low-precision arithmetic units. Prior implementations were based on INT8 and FP8, and the use of the faster FP4 had not been realized. The key property is that every FP4 value becomes an integer when doubled, and that shifting this integer set by multiples of 13 covers all integers. Converting an arbitrary integer into base-13 FP4 limbs by this property keeps intermediate sums error-free in FP32 accumulators, which makes FP4 Tensor Cores usable for Ozaki schemes I and II. By the same principle, the integer GEMM of INT8 Tensor Cores can also be emulated bit-exactly on FP4 Tensor Cores. When FP4 Tensor Cores have twice the throughput of FP8, Ozaki scheme II on FP4 theoretically achieves slightly higher performance than its FP8 counterpart. This paper further proposes kernel implementation optimizations raising the attained fraction of peak performance, obtaining a measured speedup on top of the theoretical advantage. We verify this on an RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, achieving performance competitive with that of an existing FP8-based implementation of Ozaki scheme II, and actually exceeding it at a large problem size ($16384^3$).

</details>


### [32] [StateFlow: Sequence Pipeline Parallelism for Long-Context Modeling with Linear Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06838)
*Wenxuan Zhao,Yingfa Chen,Xu Han,Wenjing Han,Tianbo Huang,Zhiyu Li,Ao Sun,Jingheng Xu,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StateFlow是一个面向线性循环模型的序列流水线并行系统，通过将序列分块执行并传播边界状态和梯度，减少激活生命周期，在32B参数、256K上下文长度的模型上实现了最高2.22倍吞吐提升和2.45倍内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文训练日益重要，线性注意力和状态空间模型虽能提升长序列效率，但如何高效并行化递归和混合模型的长序列训练仍具挑战性。

Method: StateFlow将每个序列划分为多个块并调度其执行，同时在块间传播边界状态和梯度以缩短激活生命周期；采用基于性能剖析的非均匀分块策略来平衡混合模型中递归与softmax注意力的计算；将并行度有限的状鶲转移与周围计算重叠执行。

Result: 在最高32B参数和256K上下文长度的模型上，相较于传统流水线并行，实现了最高2.22倍吞吐提升和2.45倍内存降低，使得原本不可行的配置得以支持。

Conclusion: StateFlow通过序列分块调度、非均匀分块和状态转移重叠等机制，有效解决了长序列递归/混合模型训练中的并行效率与内存瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Long-context training is increasingly important for large language models, and linear attention and state space models have become popular for improving long-context efficiency. However, efficiently parallelizing long-sequence training for recurrent and hybrid models remains challenging.
  We present StateFlow, a sequence pipeline parallelism system for models with linear recurrence. StateFlow partitions each sequence into chunks and schedules their execution while propagating boundary states and gradients across chunks, thereby reducing activation lifetimes and improving training throughput. StateFlow further uses profile-guided nonuniform chunking to balance recurrence and softmax attention computation in hybrid models, and overlaps state transitions that expose limited parallelism with surrounding computation. Applying StateFlow to models with up to 32B parameters and 256K context length, we achieve up to \(2.22\times\) throughput improvements and \(2.45\times\) memory reduction compared to conventional pipeline parallelism, enabling otherwise infeasible configurations.

</details>


### [33] [Stream Learning: Partition-Fair Gossip Learning Without Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06946)
*Fabien Mathieu,Alexandre Pham,Maria Gradinariu Potop-Butucaru,S{é}bastien Tixeuil*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文借鉴P2P直播流媒体中视频块稀缺性的思想，将八卦学习中的模型分区调度问题类比为视频块分发问题，提出了十种称为Stream Learning的协议。其中最简单的协议Ri（将本地训练最不充分的分区发送给随机邻居）在无故障场景下与SOTA的PTGL性能持平，且在30%节点永久崩溃的攻击场景下全面超越PTGL，在HAR和MNIST数据集上分别领先5.53%和5.41%。实验表明分区公平性（由单个局部规则控制）是性能差距的关键，基于令牌的速率控制和效用最大化策略并无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的八卦学习SOTA协议PTGL使用固定分区划分和基于令牌的公平机制，配合邻居元数据交换来实现模型聚合，其调度机制复杂且需要大量元数据。作者希望探索更简单有效的分区调度策略，借鉴P2P直播流中视频块稀缺性调度思想，检验是否可以用简单的局部规则替代复杂的令牌与元数据机制，同时提升在节点故障下的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者将八卦学习中的模型分区类比为P2P直播流中的视频块，将分区年龄类比为块稀缺性，构建了二维设计空间（先选分区或先选邻居），从中实例化出十种具体的Stream Learning协议。最核心的协议Ri策略是：每个节点将本地训练最不充分（年龄最小）的模型分区发送给随机选择的邻居，无需令牌计数器、无需邻居间元数据交换。作者在完整图配置下进行了大量实验，覆盖无故障场景和30%最佳性能节点永久崩溃的对抗场景，并在不同数据异构程度（Dirichlet分布）下评估。

Result: Ri协议在无故障场景下与PTGL性能持平；在30%节点永久崩溃的对抗场景下，Ri在所有完整图配置上匹配或超越PTGL，在HAR上领先达5.53%，在MNIST上领先达5.41%（Dirichlet β=0.1最强异构场景）。实验表明分区公平性（由分区年龄的单个局部规则实现）是造成性能差距的核心因素，基于令牌的速率控制和效用最大化方法未能超越该简单规则，在异构场景下甚至低于它。

Conclusion: 最复杂的机制不一定最优：一个仅依赖分区年龄的简单局部规则（Ri协议）可以在无故障时匹配SOTA性能，在节点故障时显著超越SOTA。分区公平性是八卦学习中调度效果的关键决定因素，令牌和元数据交换机制带来的复杂性收益甚微，在异构和故障场景下反而成为劣势。

Abstract: In gossip learning, a network of nodes trains a shared model collaboratively, without a central coordinator, by repeatedly exchanging parts of their local models. The state-of-the-art protocol, Partitioned Token Gossip Learning (PTGL) of Heged{ü}s et al., splits the weight matrix into S fixed partitions and disseminates them using a token-based fairness mechanism coupled with per-neighbor metadata exchange. We revisit partition scheduling by analogy with peer-to-peer live streaming, where model partitions act as video chunks and partition age acts as chunk scarcity. The analogy yields a design space of two-stage selection strategies (partition first, or neighbor first), from which we instantiate ten concrete protocols collectively called Stream Learning. Our main finding is that the simplest of these protocols, which transmits the locally least-trained partition to a uniformly random neighbor (Ri), matches PTGL on fault-free workloads while requiring neither token counters nor metadata exchange. Under an adversarial 30% permanent crash of the best-performing nodes, Ri matches or outperforms PTGL across all complete-graph configurations tested, with the gap reaching 5.53% on HAR and 5.41% on MNIST in the most heterogeneous regime (Dirichlet $β$ = 0.1). In our experiments, partition fairness, captured by a single local rule on partition age, accounts for the gap; token-based rate control and utility maximization do not improve over this rule and, under heterogeneity, sit below it.

</details>


### [34] [A Self-Adaptive Extensible CEP framework for the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06966)
*Olaf Markus Link,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的自适应复杂事件处理（CEP）框架，具备负载调节功能，帮助算子在面对过载时继续工作，从而支持算子部署在异构和/或资源受限的环境中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CEP系统处理简单事件以检测复杂事件模式，但算子图分布在云边连续体的异构节点上时，会面临动态变化的工作负载和不均衡的负载分布，导致个别算子处理过载而无法适应增长的负载，迫切需要一种能实时检测和适应动态工作负载的框架。

Method: 提出一个新的可扩展自适应性CEP框架，集成负载调节（load regulatory）特性，使算子在过载情况下仍能继续工作。

Result: 该框架能够帮助算子在面对过载时保持运行，促进了算子在异构和/或资源受限环境中的部署。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应CEP框架能实时应对云边连续体各节点动态变化的工作负载，解决了异构节点间负载不均衡带来的处理过载问题。

Abstract: Traditional complex event processing (CEP) systems focus on extracting information out of simple events in the input event streams to detect complex event patterns. In the context of CEP, a computing unit that executes a complex data transformation task is called an operator. Typically, a CEP system consists of an operator graph that could be distributed over the cloud-edge continuum. However, an operator graph distributed over heterogeneous nodes comes with its own set of challenges. In fact, the nodes of an operator graph are typically subjected to dynamically changing workloads. Unbalanced load distributions across heterogeneous nodes may lead to processing overloads in individual operators that do not have enough resources to properly adapt to an increasing workload. As a result, a framework that can detect and adapt to dynamically changing workload in real time across the various nodes of the cloud-edge continuum becomes paramount. To this end, the goal of this work is to propose a new extensible self-adaptive CEP framework with load regulatory features that help operators continue working even in face of an overload, thus facilitating the deployment of operators in a heterogeneous and/or resource-limited environment.

</details>


### [35] [A Kubernetes Scheduler Plugin for Cluster-Wide Placement Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06987)
*Henrik Daniel Christensen,Saverio Giallorenzo,Jacopo Mauro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出OPSche，一个开源的Kubernetes调度框架插件，允许外部求解器与默认调度器协同驱动集群级放置决策。OPSche通过协调框架钩子原子性地验证和执行求解器生成的计划，支持三种触发模式（调度失败、周期性、稳定队列），并配备约束优化求解器，实验表明资源利用率提升最高3.0%，调度延迟减少超过一秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes默认调度器采用快速本地放置决策，导致资源碎片化、集群利用率降低和过度配置。外部求解器虽能计算全局放置计划，但在上游集群中强制执行这些计划很困难，因为Kubernetes缺乏原生跨节点抢占、无协调的并发调度会导致不一致，且替换默认调度器会使部署脱离上游更新周期。

Method: OPSche作为Kubernetes Scheduling Framework插件，通过协调的框架钩子原子性地验证和执行外部求解器产生的放置计划，与默认调度器协同工作。支持三种触发模式：调度失败、周期性和稳定队列，每种模式还有阻塞变体用于精细调整放置质量、延迟和破坏性。

Result: 将OPSche与基于约束的优化求解器配对，在各种集群配置下验证了可行性，资源利用率提升最高3.0%，调度延迟减少超过一秒。

Conclusion: OPSche有效弥合了外部全局求解器与Kubernetes默认调度器之间的鸿沟，能够在无需替换默认调度器的情况下实现全局优化的放置决策，同时保持与上游Kubernetes的兼容性。

Abstract: The default scheduler of Kubernetes, the state-of-the-art container orchestrator, uses fast, local placement decisions. Unfortunately, this design leads to resource fragmentation, reduced cluster usage, and overprovisioning. External solvers can compute global placement plans, but enforcing these plans in upstream clusters is hard. Kubernetes provides no native cross-node preemption, uncoordinated concurrent scheduling leads to inconsistencies, and replacing the default scheduler would sever deployments from upstream cycles. We present OPSche, an open-source Kubernetes Scheduling Framework plugin where external solvers can drive cluster-wide placement decisions in concert with the default scheduler. OPSche atomically validates and enforces solver-produced plans through coordinated framework hooks and supports three trigger modes: scheduling-failure, periodic, and stable-queue -- resp. triggered when a workload cannot be placed, at fixed time intervals, when the set of pending workloads stabilises. Each mode has a blocking variant for a finer tuning of placement quality, latency, and disruption. We pair OPSche with a constraint-based optimisation solver, showing its feasibility across a broad set of cluster configurations and reporting improvements of resource usage by up to 3.0% and scheduling latency by more than a second.

</details>


### [36] [HiSparse: Scaling Sparse-Attention Decoding with Hierarchical KV Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07009)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Zhangheng Huang,Tingwei Huang,Ziyi Xu,Ruiyang Ma,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HiSparse是一个精确的、索引器无关的分层KV缓存系统，用于稀疏注意力服务。它将完整KV历史存储在主机内存中，用固定的GPU小缓存限制解码内存占用，通过融合CUDA内核处理命中检测、LRU替换和主机到设备的数据获取，已在SGLang中上线，在H200/B200/GH200平台上将长上下文峰值生成吞吐量提升高达4.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k稀疏注意力虽然降低了长上下文LLM解码的计算成本，但现有服务系统仍将完整KV缓存放在GPU HBM中，导致内存账单随上下文长度增长，解码在计算耗尽之前就遭遇容量墙，甚至超出HBM的上下文根本无法服务。

Method: HiSparse采用分层KV缓存架构：完整KV历史保存在主机内存中，GPU端仅保留固定大小的小缓存。其核心包括：1）融合CUDA内核在解码CUDA图内完成每层的选择解析（命中检测、LRU替换、主机到设备获取）；2）对于跨层共享选择的模型，利用精确的逐层预取来隐藏约一半的剩余未命中开销。由于仅改变了KV的放置位置，模型输出保持不变。

Result: HiSparse已在SGLang上游合并，并在H200、B200和GH200平台上对三种稀疏注意力家族（DSA、NSA和Quest）进行评估：长上下文工作负载的峰值生成吞吐量提升最高达4.7倍，每token延迟相当，高负载下首token时间（TTFT）降低。无IO的Oracle实验表明解析机制本身没有引入可测量的每token开销。

Conclusion: HiSparse证明以主机-设备IO为唯一代价的有界驻留（bounded residency）方案是可行的，有效突破了稀疏注意力服务中的GPU内存容量瓶颈，为长上下文LLM的高效服务提供了实用且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Top-k sparse attention makes long-context LLM decoding cheap to compute: each step reads only a few thousand selected KV entries rather than the full context. Serving systems, however, typically keep the entire KV cache in GPU HBM so that every position stays selectable, so a request's memory bill still grows with its full context length--decoding hits a capacity wall long before it runs out of compute, and a context whose KV cache exceeds HBM cannot be served at all. We present HiSparse, an exact, indexer-agnostic hierarchical KV cache for sparse-attention serving. HiSparse keeps each request's full KV history in host memory and bounds its decode footprint with a small, fixed-size GPU cache; a fused CUDA kernel resolves each layer's selections--hit detection, LRU replacement, and host-to-device fetches--inside the decode CUDA graph; and, for models that share selections across layers, exact layer-wise prefetching hides roughly half of the remaining miss overhead. Because only KV placement changes, model outputs are unchanged. HiSparse is merged into upstream SGLang and evaluated across three sparse-attention families (DSA, NSA, and Quest) on H200, B200, and GH200 platforms: it improves peak generation throughput by up to 4.7x on long-context workloads while preserving comparable per-token latency and reducing time-to-first-token at high load--and a no-IO oracle shows the resolution mechanism itself adds no measurable per-token cost, leaving host-device IO as the only price of bounded residency.

</details>

*Xiangyu Meng,Peng Chen,Mingzhen Li,Jianmin Wang,Sen Wang,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia,Mohamed Wahib,Tao Luo,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SparkleDock，一个基于GSO的可扩展柔性分子对接框架，通过在GPU上实现细粒度并行、张量核心优化的能量计算和性能模型驱动的负载均衡，实现了近实时的柔性对接，相比LightDock在单卡上获得9.7-18.9倍加速，在512卡上将对接时间从小时级缩短到秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性大分子对接在生物分子相互作用预测中具有高精度优势，但计算成本高昂。现有方法LightDock虽基于GSO具有较高精度，但存在并行度有限、计算不规则和负载严重不均衡等问题，难以在GPU超级计算机上高效执行，阻碍了大规模高保真虚拟筛选的应用。

Method: 1) 重新设计GSO算法，在萤火虫智能体层面暴露大规模细粒度并行的机会；2) 将占主导的能耗评分计算重构为兼容张量核心(Tensor Core)的矩阵运算形式，通过结构化矩阵操作高效处理不规则配对交互；3) 引入性能模型驱动的调度策略，实现GPU间的负载均衡和核外（out-of-core）扩展。

Result: 在单块A100和H100 GPU上分别比LightDock快9.7倍和18.9倍；在大规模集群上实现超过两个数量级的加速；在512块GPU上，将对接时间从数小时降至数秒。

Conclusion: SparkleDock成功解决了柔性分子对接在GPU超算上的可扩展性瓶颈，使大规模高保真虚拟筛选从不可行变为可行，为柔性对接在实际药物发现和生物分子研究中的广泛应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Flexible macromolecular docking offers high-fidelity predictions of biomolecular interactions, but remains prohibitively expensive at scale. Among existing approaches, LightDock leverages Glowworm Swarm Optimization (GSO) for accuracy, yet suffers from limited parallelism, irregular computation, and severe load imbalance, preventing efficient execution on GPU supercomputers. We present SparkleDock, a scalable GSO-based docking framework enabling near-real-time flexible docking. We redesign GSO to expose massive fine-grained parallelism at the glowworm-agent level, and restructure the dominant energy scoring computation into a Tensor Core-compatible formulation, enabling efficient execution of irregular pairwise interactions through structured matrix operations. We further introduce a performance-model-driven scheduling for load balancing and out-of-core scaling across GPUs. SparkleDock achieves 9.7 $\times$ and 18.9 $\times$ speedups over LightDock on single A100 and H100 GPU, and delivers over two orders of magnitude acceleration at scale. On 512 GPUs, it reduces docking time from hours to seconds, enabling large-scale, high-fidelity virtual screening previously impractical with flexible docking.

</details>


### [38] [Fast end-to-end cloud application cold-start with initscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07358)
*Ariel Szekely,Robert Morris,M. Frans Kaashoek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出initscripts机制，允许开发者向云平台描述应用的初始化例程，通过重叠setup和initialization步骤来减少Serverless函数的冷启动延迟，平均提速1.67X-1.81X。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless函数常为短时运行且频繁冷启动，启动延迟往往主导执行延迟。启动延迟分为平台侧setup和应用侧initialization两部分，这两部分目前按顺序执行，导致整体启动缓慢。

Method: 提出initscripts，为开发者提供脚本化接口，向云平台说明应用的初始化流程。平台可并行执行setup和initialization步骤以降低冷启动延迟，应用运行后通过initscript将初始化结果传递给应用完成引导。

Result: 在多个ServerlessBench Python应用上冷启动平均提速1.67X（无需修改应用），服务器无服务器图像识别负载提速1.81X，etcd和memcached等现成微服务也有加速。

Conclusion: initscripts能够有效降低Serverless冷启动延迟，且对现有应用无需修改即可获得显著性能提升，同时适用于各类微服务工作负载。

Abstract: Serverless functions are a popular way of deploying cloud applications. Because many of these functions are short- running and experience frequent cold-starts, start latencies often dominate their execution latency. Start latency can be broken down into two components: setup and initialization. Setup involves steps the cloud platform takes when starting an application, such as downloading its binary and creating an isolated execution environment. Initialization involves steps the application takes after it has started running but before it can do useful work, such as connecting to other services, coordinating to claim work, and downloading inputs.
  This paper contributes initscripts, which provide a script- able interface for developers to specify their application's initialization routine to the cloud platform. The platform can then run an application's initscript and reduce application start latency by overlapping setup and initialization steps. Once the application is up and running, the initscript bootstraps the application by transferring initialization results to it. Using initscripts, we were able to speed up cold-starts of several of the ServerlessBench Python applications by an average 1.67X with no modifications to the application. Initscripts also speed up start times for a serverless image recognition workload by 1.81X, and off-the-shelf microservices like etcd and memcached.

</details>

### [39] [Latent Fact-Checking: Detecting Misinformation through Activation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06417)
*Pedro Barcelos,Otávio Parraga,Marcelo M. Mussi,Lucas M. Fraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于激活工程的虚假信息检测框架，通过在大语言模型的残差流中对比真假陈述的激活差异来提取"虚假方向"，推理时将最后一个token的激活投影到该方向并输入MLP进行分类，无需微调或外部知识检索。在11个模型（Gemma、Llama、Qwen，270M到12B参数）和三个基准（AVeriTeC、LIAR、FACTors）上的实验表明，该方法在LIAR和FACTors上匹配或超越零样本/少样本提示基线，尤其对小模型增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测多依赖表面语言特征或外部知识检索，缺乏对模型内部表征几何结构的利用。作者希望验证真实性是否可以作为预训练语言模型隐空间中结构化的、线性可分的概念，从而提供一种无需微调和外部证据的检测方法。

Method: 基于对比激活添加（CAA）的差均值原理，通过配对的真实与虚假陈述激活对比提取残差流中的"虚假信息方向"。推理时，将未见声明的最后一个token激活投影到该方向，再将投影表示输入MLP进行分类。该方法无需微调骨干模型，无需外部证据检索，仅需对比对来估计方向。

Result: 在11个模型和3个基准上的评估表明：虚假方向在多种模型规模和架构族中均可恢复；最后一token投影在LIAR和FACTors上匹配或超越零样本和少样本提示基线，小模型的增益最大；AVeriTeC上表现有限，归因于其基于证据的标注方案。

Conclusion: 研究为真实性是预训练语言模型隐空间中结构化且线性可分的概念提供了证据，表明可解释性驱动的虚假信息检测可以作为基于检索管道的实用补充方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of misinformation online has driven demand for scalable detection systems. While most existing approaches rely on surface-level linguistic features or external knowledge retrieval, we examine truthfulness as a geometric property of a language model's representation space. We introduce a misinformation detection framework grounded in activation engineering, which leverages the latent geometry of transformer models. Our approach elicits a misinformation direction in the residual stream by contrasting activations from paired truthful and false statements, following the difference-in-means principle of Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA). At inference time, the last-token activation of an unseen claim is projected onto this direction, and the projected representation is fed to an Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for classification. The procedure requires no fine-tuning of the backbone model, no external evidence retrieval, and no task-specific supervision beyond the contrastive pairs used to estimate the direction. We evaluate the method across 11 models from the Gemma, Llama, and Qwen families, ranging from 270M to 12B parameters, on three fact-checking benchmarks: AVeriTeC, LIAR, and FACTors. The falsehood direction is recoverable across model scales and architectural families, and last-token projection matches or surpasses zero-shot and few-shot prompting baselines on LIAR and FACTors, with the largest gains observed for smaller models. Performance on AVeriTeC is more limited, which we attribute to its evidence-grounded labeling scheme. These findings provide evidence that truthfulness is a structured, linearly separable concept in the latent space of pretrained language models, and point toward interpretability-driven misinformation detection as a practical complement to retrieval-based pipelines. The code is available on https://github.com/Malta-Lab/LaFaCt.

</details>


### [40] [Risk-Aware Decision Policies for Agents Under Noisy Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06420)
*David Szczecina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个人工生命捕食者-猎物模型，在噪声感知条件下比较不同决策策略，发现不确定性感知策略能显著提升生物体在噪声环境中的生存率，并观察到从探索性到保守性的行为范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统的感知天然存在噪声，不确定性下的决策失误可能致命，而现有研究缺乏对噪声感知下不同决策策略的系统比较。

Method: 构建人工生命捕食者-猎物觅食模型，在对称和非对称感知噪声条件下，对比盲目信任感知标签的策略与各种不确定性感知策略的生存表现。

Result: 盲目信任感知标签在噪声增大时导致灾难性失败，而不确定性感知策略显著改善生存率并减少致命错误；同时观察到行为随不确定性增加从探索性向保守性的定性范式转变。

Conclusion: 显式信息收集能提升感知不可靠时的鲁棒性，该模型为噪声标签下的鲁棒学习提供了可解释的人工生命类比，强调了不确定性感知决策的重要性。

Abstract: Perception in biological systems is inherently noisy, requiring organisms to make decisions under uncertainty where misclassification can be costly or fatal. We present an Artificial Life predator-prey model of foraging under noisy perception, and compare agent performance when using various policies that take into account their noisy predictions. Through controlled experiments under both symmetric and asymmetric perceptual noise, we show that blindly trusting perceptual labels leads to catastrophic failure as noise increases, while uncertainty-aware strategies significantly improve survival and reduce fatal errors. We further observe qualitative regime shifts in behaviour, with agents transitioning from exploratory to conservative strategies as uncertainty increases. Our model links risk-sensitive foraging, ecological information use, and Artificial Life by showing that explicit information gathering can improve robustness when perception is unreliable. These results highlight the importance of uncertainty-aware decision-making and provide an interpretable artificial life analogue to robust learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [41] [Sharding Prevents LLM Oversight Failures and Adversarial Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06422)
*Victor Akinwande,J. Zico Kolter,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM评判者在一个调用中处理多个判定时，其决策的证据基础会变弱，导致与专家一致性下降。作者提出"分片"（sharding）干预措施，将需求分组后分别调用评判，在保持模型、证据和总预算不变的情况下提升评判质量，且分片后的弱评判者能超越更强大的整体评判者。分片还具备对抗鲁棒性，能消除利用评判过载的对抗者的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评判范式常让单一调用返回多个判定，但研究表明更多计算并不必然带来更多需求检查——当一次调用需返回多个判定时，部分决策的证据基础薄弱，导致与专家意见的一致性下降。需要找出缓解这种"评判过载"失败的干预方法。

Method: 提出"分片"干预：将需求划分为更小的分组，每组分配给独立的评判调用，最后聚合判定结果。通过专家分级的研究复制、法律工作和临床试验评估来验证效果，并测试最佳N对抗者（best-of-N adversary）的鲁棒性，同时探索辩论式对抗（debate-style opposition）以应对逐条说服型攻击。

Result: 分片在保持模型、证据、总预算和单决策预算不变的情况下提升与专家的一致性；分片的弱评判者可超越更强的整体评判者，甚至与获得全部预算的更强者持平。分片能消除利用过载的攻击优势，但在逐条说服型攻击下失效，需叠加辩论式对抗才能抵御自适应重新优化。

Conclusion: 分片是缓解LLM评判过载的有效手段，兼具性能提升与对抗鲁棒性。但面对逐条说服型对手时需结合辩论式机制，这表明不同攻击类型需要不同的防御策略，单靠分片无法全面解决问题。

Abstract: Giving an LLM judge more compute does not necessarily make it check more requirements. When one call must return many verdicts, some decisions become weakly grounded in the evidence, even when that call receives the same token or tool budget as a panel of separate calls. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. We identify sharding as the intervention that mitigates this failure in model-based oversight. Sharding partitions the requirements into smaller groups, assigns each group to a separate call, and aggregates the verdicts. Against a single call with the panel's full budget, sharding improves agreement while holding the model, evidence, total budget, and per-decision budget fixed. Overall, we find that a sharded weaker judge can outperform a more capable holistic judge and match that judge even when the latter receives the panel's full budget. Additionally, we find that sharding exhibits robustness against adversaries. A best-of-N adversary can hold the underlying work fixed, vary only its presentation, and increase an overloaded judge's acceptance of genuinely unmet criteria severalfold. Wherever sharding reduces baseline error, it removes this adversarial advantage, keeping over-acceptance low even as the adversary's search widens. Sharding does not address attacks that persuade the judge separately on each criterion rather than exploiting overload. In that setting, we find that debate-style opposition on top of sharding withstands such adaptive re-optimization.

</details>


### [42] [Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06427)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性因果干预伪造检验（ACIF）框架，通过一个对抗性实验者选择干预来最大程度地伪造结构因果生成器的输出分布，从而区分观察拟合、干预等价性和结构因果模型的点识别这三个常被混淆的概念。作者证明了该对抗目标可精确约化为最差干预下的积分概率度量，并给出了识别性、均衡存在性、有限样本收敛性及序贯设计下的对数消除保证等理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可以重现观测分布的同时却编码了错误的因果结构，现有的对抗生成框架中判别器仅作为真实与合成样本的分类器，无法验证模型的因果正确性。作者希望构建一个能够测试生成器在干预后分布上是否与真实系统一致的对抗性框架，从而澄清对抗性因果判别器能证明和不能证明什么。

Method: 提出ACIF序贯博弈框架：结构因果生成器提出观测分布和干预分布，对抗性实验者选择干预来最大程度地伪造生成器。判别器由干预索引，测试生成器是否重现对应的干预后分布。作者制定了oracle和可实现的博弈版本，并区分了观测拟合、可查询类上的干预等价性和结构因果模型的点识别这三个对象。

Result: 对于有限的模型和干预类别，证明了：(i) 对抗目标可精确约化为最坏干预的积分概率度量；(ii) 在干预等价意义下可识别，在分离干预族下可点识别；(iii) 混合策略均衡存在；(iv) 有限样本一致收敛和基于间隔的模型选择保证；(v) 在平衡分离条件下的分歧驱动序贯设计具有对数消除保证。还给出了线性高斯示例，其中两个观测不可区分的因果方向由单个精心选择的干预分离。

Conclusion: 该框架阐明了对抗性因果判别器能且不能验证的内容，为因果生成建模、主动因果发现和实验设计之间提供了原则性的桥梁。

Abstract: Generative models can reproduce an observational distribution while encoding an incorrect causal structure. We study a sequential game in which a structural causal generator proposes observational and interventional distributions, while an adversarial experimentalist selects interventions intended to maximally falsify the generator. The discriminator is therefore not merely a real-versus-synthetic classifier: it is indexed by an intervention and tests whether the generator reproduces the corresponding post-intervention law. We introduce Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification (ACIF), formulate oracle and implementable versions of the game, and distinguish three objects that are often conflated: observational fit, interventional equivalence over an admissible query class, and point identification of a structural causal model. For finite model and intervention classes, we prove: (i) an exact reduction of the adversarial objective to a worst-intervention integral probability metric; (ii) identification up to interventional equivalence, with point identification under a separating intervention family; (iii) existence of mixed-strategy equilibria; (iv) finite-sample uniform convergence and margin-based model-selection guarantees; and (v) a logarithmic elimination guarantee for a disagreement-driven sequential design under a balanced-separation condition. We also give a complete linear-Gaussian example in which two observationally indistinguishable causal directions are separated by a single well-chosen intervention. The framework clarifies what an adversarial causal discriminator can and cannot certify, and provides a principled bridge between causal generative modeling, active causal discovery, and experimental design.

</details>


### [43] [Fixed and Adaptive Topological DeepONets: Functional Measurements on Hausdorff Locally Convex Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06428)
*Khemraj Shukla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Topological DeepONet框架基础上，将输入函数的点采样替换为来自Hausdorff局部凸空间连续对偶空间的连续线性泛函，开发了固定和自适应功能测量系统，并结合Two-Step过程和训练专用解码器。在多个算子学习任务（反导数、异构Darcy流、受控算子、Navier-Stokes涡量算子）上验证了该方法的有效性，实现了分辨率无关的误差和紧凑可解释的坐标表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DeepONet通过固定离散化上的点值编码输入函数，受限于离散化网格并缺乏对非赋范空间的适应性。作者希望利用更一般的局部凸空间中的连续线性泛函作为测量手段，突破传统点采样的限制，实现对非赋范输入空间的支持以及更紧凑、可解释、跨离散化可移植的坐标表示。

Method: 基于Topological DeepONet框架，将输入通过来自Hausdorff局部凸空间连续对偶空间的连续线性泛函进行编码，开发固定和自适应的功能测量系统；结合Lee和Shin的系数空间Two-Step过程，使用训练专用解码器和正则化稳定自适应坐标；推导了分离测量误差、输出基误差和神经逼近误差的离散误差分解，并提供Barron率细化。

Result: 在异构Darcy流问题中，功能模型在未见网格上保持5.5-5.6%的近分辨率无关误差；受控问题中自适应测量将平均误差降至1.2%以下；固定时间Navier-Stokes问题中，自适应Topological DeepONet以128个功能坐标达到1.685%±0.017%的平均相对L2误差，优于同规模FNO的0.832%±0.172%（但FNO需要完整64×64输入场、两倍训练时间和10.7倍峰值GPU内存）。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了在连续对偶空间V'中紧凑、可解释且离散化可移植的坐标表示，支持包括非赋范局部凸空间在内的输入空间，在计算效率、内存占用和坐标解释性方面优于传统的基于点采样的方法。

Abstract: Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets; arXiv:1910.03193) typically encode an input function through point values on a fixed discretization. Building on the Topological DeepONet framework of Ismailov (arXiv:2603.11972), we replace point samples by continuous linear functionals drawn from the continuous dual of a Hausdorff locally convex space $({V},\{p_α\}_{α\in A})$, whose topology is generated by a point-separating family of seminorms rather than a single norm, and develop fixed and adaptive functional measurement systems. Measurements are combined with the coefficient-space Two-Step procedure of Lee and Shin (arXiv:2309.01020), while a training-only decoder and regularization stabilize the adaptive coordinates. We derive a discrete error decomposition separating measurement, output-basis, and neural-approximation errors, together with a Barron-rate refinement. The framework is evaluated on the antiderivative operator, a non-normable locally convex input space, heterogeneous Darcy flow, a controlled operator, and fixed-time and time-evolving Navier-Stokes vorticity operators. In the heterogeneous Darcy problem, the functional models retain nearly resolution-independent errors of 5.5-5.6% on unseen grids, while in the controlled problem adaptive measurements reduce the mean error below 1.2%. For the fixed-time Navier-Stokes problem, the Adaptive Topological DeepONet is the most accurate DeepONet-based model, attaining a mean relative $L^2$ error of 1.685% +/- 0.017% using 128 functional coordinates. A comparably sized Fourier neural operator (FNO; arXiv:2010.08895) achieves the lower error 0.832% +/- 0.172%, but requires the full 64x64 input field, twice the training time, and 10.7x greater peak GPU memory. The formulation provides compact, interpretable, and discretization-portable coordinates in the continuous dual $V'$, including for non-normable input spaces.

</details>


### [44] [MiGHT-EHR: A Multi-task Graph Transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06430)
*Anirudh Rayas,Yuan Wang,Pavan Turaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MiGHT-EHR，一种用于异质时序电子健康记录（EHR）的多任务图Transformer框架，统一建模异质临床实体交互、纵向患者轨迹及多任务共享依赖，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上多项临床预测任务中超越现有方法，且学习到的表征具有良好的临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR学习方法只能捕获异质性、时序性和多任务共享性这三个关键属性中的一部分，缺乏一个能够同时建模所有属性的统一框架，导致临床预测的性能受限。

Method: MiGHT-EHR构建了一个异质图，节点表示临床实体（患者、就诊、诊断、处方、程序），通过归一化逐点互信息（NPMI）识别统计相关的实体并建立边；采用图Transformer联合建模异质交互、纵向轨迹和多任务共享依赖，在统一表征学习框架内进行端到端训练。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，MiGHT-EHR在药物推荐、住院时长预测、死亡率预测和再入院预测四项任务上均优于现有最先进方法，尤其在死亡率和再入院预测上提升显著。事后分析显示患者邻居按临床结局组织，重要医学概念可作为表征空间中的线性方向恢复，任务概率校准良好。

Conclusion: MiGHT-EHR通过统一建模EHR的异质性、时序性和多任务共享性，实现了更优的临床预测性能，同时学习到的表征保留了临床可解释结构，能够支持多样化预测任务并具备临床实用性。

Abstract: Learning from Electronic Health Records (EHRs) has gained significant attention due to its potential to improve clinical prediction. However, effective learning remains challenging because EHRs encode heterogeneous, temporally ordered clinical interactions. In particular, EHRs contain: (i) heterogeneous clinical entities, including patients, visits, diagnoses, prescriptions, and procedures, together with their heterogeneous interactions, (ii) longitudinal patient trajectories across hospital visits and (iii) shared statistical dependencies across related clinical prediction tasks. Existing EHR learning methods capture only a subset of these properties. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-task Graph transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal EHRs (MiGHT-EHR), which jointly models all three within a unified representation learning method. MiGHT-EHR constructs a heterogeneous graph from EHRs in which nodes represent clinical entities and edges connect statistically associated entities identified via normalized point-wise mutual information. Across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, MiGHT-EHR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on average across four tasks: drug recommendation, prediction of length-of-stay, mortality, and readmission, with particularly strong improvements in mortality and readmission prediction. Furthermore, a post-hoc analysis of the learned representations reveals that patient neighborhoods are organized by clinical outcomes, salient medical concepts are recoverable as linear directions in the representation space, and task probabilities are well calibrated. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that MiGHT-EHR representations support diverse prediction tasks while preserving clinically interpretable structure.

</details>


### [45] [SNI-GNN: SmartNIC-Assisted Full-Graph GNN Training with In-Network Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06441)
*Guofan Yu,Sitian Chen,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNI-GNN是一个基于SmartNIC辅助的全图GNN训练系统，通过在网络内预测远程嵌入来减少通信开销，在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现21-45%的通信削减和1.3-3.6倍端到端加速，同时保持精度损失不超过0.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图GNN训练虽然精度高，但在多服务器集群上因节点间频繁且不规则的嵌入交换而扩展性差，通信成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 在SmartNIC上部署轻量级线性趋势预测器来细化缓存的历史嵌入，结合基于重要性的边界节点采样策略，以及异步DPU-GPU数据流水线和中间结果复用机制。

Result: 在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现：通信减少21-45%，相比BNS-GCN获得1.3-3.6倍端到端加速，相比SANCUS基线最高1.29倍加速，精度损失不超过0.01，可扩展到16个GPU和数千万条边的图。

Conclusion: 基于SmartNIC的网络内预测是分区和压缩技术之外的一种实用补充方案，可用于大规模通信高效的全图GNN训练。

Abstract: Full-graph GNN training delivers high accuracy but scales poorly on multi-server clusters due to heavy, irregular inter-node embedding exchanges. We present SNI-GNN, a SmartNIC-assisted full-graph training system that reduces communication while preserving accuracy by predicting remote embeddings in-network. SNI-GNN deploys a lightweight linear-trend predictor on SmartNICs to refine cached historical embeddings, coupled with an importance-based boundary-node sampling policy and an asynchronous DPU--GPU data pipeline with intermediate-result reuse. We provide error and convergence bounds showing that predictor bias remains controlled under bounded second-order dynamics and yields standard non-convex convergence with inexact gradients. Implemented on NVIDIA BlueField-3, SNI-GNN integrates with state-of-the-art full-graph systems, cuts communication by 21--45\%, achieves 1.3--3.6$\times$ end-to-end speedups over BNS-GCN and up to 1.29$\times$ over baseline SANCUS, with accuracy loss $\leq 0.01$, and scales efficiently to 16 GPUs on graphs with up to tens of millions of edges. These results indicate SmartNIC-based in-network prediction is a practical complement to partitioning and compression techniques for communication-efficient full-graph GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [46] [ED-CSP: Crystal Structure Prediction from Electron Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06448)
*Germain Poloudenny,Yaël Frégier,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ED-CSP，一个从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射（ED）观测中生成性恢复周期性3D晶体结构的机器学习框架。该系统结合关系集合编码器、排列不变多视角聚合和周期流生成器，联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。在4.85百万模拟多视角ED晶体结构数据集（ED-CS）上训练，ED-CSP在CHILI-100K基准上达到57.49%的结构匹配率（MR@5），优于PXRDGen（52.92%）；扩大训练数据后可提升至66.27%，在训练库外的组合上仍保持53.52%的生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射观测中恢复周期性三维晶体结构是一个具有挑战性的生成逆问题。现有基于ED的学习方法主要预测晶体学标签、从索引反射中重构结构或从有限结构库中检索候选，缺乏真正的生成能力。

Method: ED-CSP包含三个核心组件：关系集合编码器处理探测器平面上的ED斑点集，排列不变多视角聚合合并多视角信息，周期流生成器联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。训练数据ED-CS包含485万模拟多视角ED晶体结构，经七个数材库去重并排除CHILI-100K重叠。

Result: 在2,075个CHILI-100K材料上，ED-CSP实现57.49% MR@5，超过PXRDGen（52.92%）。用100万结构预训练后扩展训练数据，MR@5提升至66.27%。在1,024个不在训练检索库中的组合上，仍然达到53.52% MR@5。用同组分非异构结构的衍射替换目标ED时，MR@5下降22.09个百分点，确认预测依赖输入衍射图案。

Conclusion: ED-CSP和ED-CS为从稀疏ED观测中生成性预测晶体结构建立了基准，展示了超越精确公式检索的真实生成能力，并为未来向实验数据迁移奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recovering a periodic 3D crystal structure from sparse, unindexed electron diffraction (ED) observations is a challenging generative inverse problem. Existing ED-based learning methods mainly predict crystallographic labels, reconstruct structures from indexed reflections, or retrieve candidates from finite structure libraries. Here, we introduce ED-CSP, a machine learning framework that predicts crystal structures from chemical composition, atom count, and multiple detector-plane ED spot sets. ED-CSP combines a relational set encoder, permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation, and a periodic flow generator to jointly predict lattice parameters and fractional atomic coordinates.
  To train the model, we construct ED-CS, a dataset of 4.85 million simulated multi-view ED crystal structures, deduplicated across seven materials repositories and filtered to exclude CHILI-100K overlaps. On 2,075 held-out CHILI-100K materials, ED-CSP trained only on CHILI achieves a structural match rate of 57.49% MR@5, outperforming PXRDGen (52.92%), a state-of-the-art crystal structure prediction model conditioned on powder X-ray diffraction. Scaling training data further improves performance: initializing from a one-million-structure precursor raises MR@5 to 66.27%. On 1,024 compositions absent from the training retrieval library, the model still achieves 53.52% MR@5, demonstrating true generative capability beyond exact-formula retrieval. Replacing target ED observations with diffraction from non-isomorphic structures of identical composition decreases MR@5 by 22.09 percentage points, confirming that predictions depend on the input diffraction patterns rather than composition alone. ED-CSP and ED-CS establish a benchmark for generative crystal structure prediction from sparse ED observations and provide a foundation for future transfer to experimental data.

</details>


### [47] [Beyond Attention: Signed Integrated Gradients Attribution in a BiomeGPT-Style Microbiome Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06486)
*Oren Nelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对特征化token的Transformer（如BiomeGPT）提出了一种基于Integrated Gradients的可解释性方法，通过源来源基线T' = S + A₀来分离物种身份与丰度对分类的影响，提供有符号的致病/保护性微生物信号归因，并推荐二阶Integrated Hessians揭示微生物群落交互规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于[CLS]注意力权重的解释方法存在两个关键局限：1）注意力权重非负，无法区分致病与保护性证据；2）注意力作用于token融合之后，无法厘清物种身份S和丰度A各自对输出的影响。

Method: 采用Integrated Gradients（有符号、融合感知的归因方法），提出源来源基线T'=S+A₀，该基线保留物种身份作为固定的生物坐标，同时隔离丰度变化的影响。将方法应用于疾病与健康决策边距，获得可区分致病/保护性微生物信号的极性；进一步推荐二阶Integrated Hessians来揭示微生物群落成员间的交互规则。

Result: 该方法能揭示物种-丰度的方向性关系及敏感性诊断，这些是[CLS]注意力权重完全无法捕捉的；二阶Hessians可暴露微生物群落交互机制，包括某成员扰动对其他成员敏感度的影响，以及模糊案例的驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该研究为BiomeGPT等特征化token模型提供了原理性的可解释框架，可推广至其他光滑可微的特征化token Transformer模型。

Abstract: In a feature-tokenized transformer (arXiv:2106.11959) such as BiomeGPT (doi:10.64898/2026.01.05.697599), each input token is built by fusing a fixed identity with a sample-specific measurement: a fixed species and a variable abundance, T = S + A. To interpret downstream classification in such models, prior work inspects the attention weights of the special [CLS] token (arXiv:2106.11959, arXiv:1810.04805, BiomeGPT) to rank sample tokens by importance. These weights have two critical limitations: they are nonnegative, so they cannot separate disease-supporting from health-supporting evidence (arXiv:2201.12114), and they act after token fusion, obscuring how the input sources S and A each affect the output.
  To address this we use Integrated Gradients (arXiv:1703.01365), a signed, fusion-aware attribution method, and propose a source-derived baseline T' = S + A_0 for feature-tokenized models such as BiomeGPT, which preserves species identity as a fixed biological coordinate while isolating the effect of abundance variation. Applied to a disease-versus-health decision margin, it yields polarity that explicitly separates pathogenic from protective microbial signals. We show that this gradient-based approach uncovers species-abundance directional relationships and sensitivity diagnostics entirely obscured by unsigned [CLS] attention weights. We further recommend second-order Integrated Hessians (arXiv:2002.04138) to expose microbiome community interaction rules: how a perturbation in one member alters the model's sensitivity to another, and which other species drive ambiguous cases toward disease or health at a given abundance level. This provides a principled approach to explainability in BiomeGPT that generalizes to other smooth and differentiable feature-tokenized transformers. Code is available at https://github.com/nohren/token-source-attribution

</details>


### [48] [Toward Reliable Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents: An Empirical Study of Execution Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06503)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mayank Darbari,Chen Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过初步实证研究发现，循环上下文压缩会削弱智能体对近期交互的影响，导致阻塞动作增多、重复探索和运行不稳定。为此提出TRACE框架，利用验证器引导的配对闭环续作来评估每次压缩事件，并通过摘要偏好优化自然语言压缩提示（模型保持冻结）。在AppWorld上的初步结果表明，TRACE在任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文执行效率方面均优于现有压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体中的循环上下文压缩虽然能控制上下文增长，但其行为影响尚不清楚。作者通过实证发现压缩会削弱近期交互的影响，引发一系列行为退化问题，由此需要一种更可靠的压缩评估与优化方法。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，该框架通过验证器引导，从相同环境状态出发生成配对的闭环续作来评估每个单独压缩事件，并利用摘要偏好（summary preferences）优化自然语言压缩提示，全程保持所有模型参数冻结。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的初步结果显示，TRACE在任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文执行效率三个维度上均优于现有压缩基线。

Conclusion: 这些发现为"边界局部评估"（boundary-local evaluation）这一方向提供了早期证据，表明其是构建可靠智能体上下文压缩的有前景的研究路径。

Abstract: Recurrent context compression controls context growth in long-horizon agents, but its behavioral effects remain poorly understood. In this preliminary empirical study, we show that compression can weaken the influence of recent interactions, increasing blocked actions, repeated exploration, and instability across runs. Motivated by these observations, we introduce TRACE, a verifier-guided framework that evaluates individual compaction events through paired closed-loop continuations from the same environment state and uses summary preferences to optimize a natural-language compression prompt while keeping all models frozen. Initial results on AppWorld show improvements over existing compression baselines in task performance, multi-run reliability, and context--execution efficiency. These findings provide early evidence for boundary-local evaluation as a promising direction for reliable agent context compression.

</details>


### [49] [Unmasking Removal-Budget Confounding: A Matched Operating-Point Evaluation Framework for Adaptive Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06511)
*Wei-Hsiang Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Chen-Hsuan Fang,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向操作点的评估框架，用于解决自适应数据清洗方法中"移除预算混杂"的评估偏差问题。通过在匹配预算和匹配召回率的条件下评估方法，并辅以AUROC/AUPRC等阈值无关指标，揭示了以往朴素评估中观察到的性能提升往往是评估偏差而非真实的污染判别能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应数据清洗方法用数据驱动的分区取代了手动过滤阈值，但分区粒度和分组数量的改变会隐式移动决策边界，影响被移除样本的总数（即去除预算）。这种"去除预算混杂"会导致评估指标（如精确率、假阳性率）的表观改善反映的是更小的去除预算而非更优的污染判别能力，从而产生评估偏差。

Method: 提出操作点感知的评估框架，使用匹配预算（matched-budget）和匹配召回率（matched-recall）作为对照，结合AUROC和AUPRC等阈值无关指标进行评估。同时设计了一个多线索自适应清洗器，包含重新加权学习困难线索、辅助欧氏距离线索以及增加分区粒度，用于隔离"干净但困难"样本，并通过假阳性分解分析不同净化程度下错误的来源。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100上的实验表明：朴素评估（各配置在自身诱发操作点下评估）显示重构方法有显著性能提升，但操作点对齐后这些增益消失。假阳性分解显示，干净但困难的样本在低污染率时主导错误计数，在中度污染时变得阈值依赖，在严重污染下贡献可忽略。匹配操作点后，大多数朴素评估中的性能差异在低至中度污染下缩小或消失，仅在特定低流行率场景和严重污染的高召回区域保留真正的排序优势。

Conclusion: 自适应清洗方法必须在匹配的操作点下进行基准测试，以确保性能增益反映的是真实的污染判别能力，而非去除预算的变化。现有朴素评估可能高估了自适应清洗器的实际效果。

Abstract: Adaptive data-cleaning methods replace manual filtering thresholds with data-driven partitions. However, changing the partition granularity, the number of groups used to segment samples by estimated corruption risk, can implicitly shift the decision boundary and alter the overall number of removed samples. This creates a bias known as removal-budget confounding, where apparent gains in metrics like precision or false-positive rate reflect a smaller removal budget rather than superior corruption discrimination. To address this evaluation bias, we introduce an operating-point-aware evaluation framework that evaluates methods using matched-budget and matched-recall controls alongside threshold-independent metrics (AUROC and AUPRC). We test this framework on a multi-cue adaptive cleaner redesign featuring a reweighted learning-difficulty cue, an auxiliary Euclidean-distance cue, and increased partition granularity intended to isolate clean-but-difficult samples. While naive evaluations (assessing configurations at their own induced operating points) suggest substantial performance improvements for the redesign, these gains disappear once operating points are equalized. False-positive decomposition reveals that clean-but-difficult samples primarily drive error counts at low corruption rates, become threshold-dependent at moderate corruption, and contribute negligibly under severe corruption. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that most performance differences observed in naive evaluation shrink or vanish at low-to-moderate corruption when operating points are matched. True ranking advantages only remain in specific low-prevalence settings and in high-recall regions under severe corruption. These findings highlight that adaptive cleaning methods must be benchmarked at matched operating points to ensure performance gains reflect genuine corruption discrimination.

</details>


### [50] [Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation: Experimental Design for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects under Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06512)
*Hoang Dang,Luan Pham,Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TWNA（Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation），一种两阶段分层实验设计方法，利用试点估计的组-臂结果方差来分配最终阶段样本量和处理概率，以优化目标加权组平均处理效应（GATE）的估计精度。该方法在部署重要性与统计难度之间取得平衡，并扩展至处理部署组成不确定性的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机实验通常在某个群体中开展，却用于指导另一个群体的决策。按实验比例分配会浪费预算在部署中很少出现的组别，而按部署比例分配则会对难以精确测量的组别采样不足。现有方法无法同时兼顾部署重要性与统计精确性。

Method: 提出TWNA两阶段分层设计：第一阶段用试点估计各分组-处理臂的结果方差，第二阶段据此分配样本量及处理概率。Oracle规则具有闭式解，能平衡部署重要性与统计难度；plug-in规则在试点方差估计稳定后可恢复Oracle规则。此外，TWNA扩展至部署组成不确定（大致已知或完全未知）的情形，并区分权重鲁棒性与针对偏态、稀有事件或污染结果的试点鲁棒变体。

Result: 通过模拟实验和真实协变量基准测试，TWNA在组别既部署重要又难以测量时获得最大的精度提升。

Conclusion: TWNA提供了一种系统化的分层实验分配策略，在部署重要性与统计可测性之间实现最优平衡，并具备应对部署组成不确定性的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Randomized experiments are often run in one population to guide decisions in another. Allocating by experimental proportions wastes budget on groups that rarely appear in deployment, whereas allocating by deployment proportions under-samples groups that are hard to measure precisely. We propose \textbf{TWNA} (Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation), a two-stage stratified design that uses pilot estimates of group--arm outcome variances to allocate final-stage sample sizes and treatment probabilities for target-weighted group average treatment effect (GATE) precision. The oracle rule has a closed form and balances deployment importance with statistical difficulty; the plug-in rule recovers it as pilot variance estimates stabilize. We also extend TWNA to handle uncertainty about deployment composition, remaining robust whether the target mix is roughly known or entirely unknown. Finally, we distinguish this weight robustness from a pilot-robust variant for skewed, rare-event, or contaminated outcomes. Simulations and real-covariate benchmarks show the largest gains when groups are both deployment-important and difficult to measure.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Weight quantization for large-language-model inference must balance adaptive reconstruction levels with representations regular enough for efficient GPU execution. Uniform integers constrain each group to a linear grid. Low-bit floating-point formats use a fixed exponent-mantissa structure, while learned codebooks gain flexibility at the cost of irregular decoding and additional metadata.
  We introduce CubicQuant, a parametric non-uniform scalar format that preserves a dense integer code stream while adapting reconstruction levels within each weight group. A monotonic cubic curve, specified by two shape parameters and one scale, maps uniformly spaced magnitude codes to non-uniform levels. The family spans 1-8-bit weight payloads, contains symmetric uniform integer quantization as an exact special case, and has effective width B + 64/G bits per weight for payload width B and group size G. We derive population distortion under Uniform, Gaussian, and Laplace distributions, formulate continuous and Dynamic-A8-carrier-aware fitting objectives, and describe direct packed-weight GPU execution.
  For finite groups of G=128 with 15,360 samples per distribution, W4 CubicQuant reduced reconstruction RMSE relative to optimally clipped four-bit uniform integer quantization by 3.90% on Uniform, 13.49% on Gaussian, and 28.14% on Laplace samples. Relative to the best enumerated four-bit finite floating-point format, the reductions were 3.90%, 9.44%, and 6.27%. Preliminary H200 kernel measurements show a workload-dependent crossover: model-dtype execution is faster for narrow GEMV, while Dynamic A8 becomes favorable as row count grows. The results establish the format's representational promise and direct executability; downstream model quality and cross-device end-to-end performance remain open evaluation questions.

</details>


### [52] [CertBind from Multimodal Connectivity to Certifiable Retrieval Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06516)
*Shuheng Cao,Zhenhao Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Renjie Cao,Weijia Zhang,Siyu Zhang,Jiaxin Liu,Xiangyu Zeng,Haotian Geng,Fan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CertBind是一个面向冻结多模态编码器连接图的多尺度可证明组合理论框架，在节点、边、路径和查询四个尺度上提供严格的认证保证，确保组合路由不会损害原有的检索能力，并能对不确定查询进行可靠裁决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级连接器使得冻结的多模态编码器在表示层面可以组合，但在任务决策层面出现了新问题：连接路由在扩展跨模态能力的同时，可能损害已建立的本地检索能力（如CLIP R@1从0.524降至0.290），需要一种能够保证组合安全性的理论框架。

Method: CertBind提出四尺度可证明组合理论：1) 节点尺度：原生锚点确立精确的任务识别边界；2) 边尺度：契约感知的共形排名提供图级族错误控制；3) 路径尺度：重叠感知预算与干净校准提供有限样本恢复半径；4) 查询尺度：恢复半径产生覆盖的top-k候选集，当大小为k时成为点证书。系统将受支持路由标记为Direct，仅将标记路由送交恢复，决定性恢复返回Certified，无法解决的查询返回Abstain。

Result: 共享的C-MCR路由将原生CLIP R@1从0.524降至0.290；生产环境回退方案恢复了0.963±0.002的干净检索性能；通过分支记录了1.000的无伤害值。

Conclusion: CertBind将多模态组合性从连接表示层面扩展到可认证的任务决策层面，使得组合路由在扩展跨模态能力的同时能够保证原有检索能力的完整性和决策的可信度。

Abstract: Lightweight connectors make frozen multimodal encoders composable at the representation level. Deployment exposes a second problem at the level of task decisions. A connected route can expand cross-modal reach while changing an established native retrieval capability. We introduce CertBind, a multiscale theory of certifiable composition for frozen multimodal connector graphs. At the node scale, native anchors establish the exact task identification boundary under the stated chart model. At the edge scale, contract-aware conformal ranks provide graph-wide family-wise error control. At the path scale, an overlap-aware budget and clean calibration yield a finite-sample recovery radius under declared conditions. At the query scale, this radius yields a covered top-k candidate set that becomes a point certificate when its size equals k. CertBind therefore retains supported routes as Direct, sends only flagged routes to recovery, returns Certified for decisive recovery, and returns Abstain for unresolved queries. The evaluated C-MCR shared route reduced native CLIP R@1 from 0.524 to 0.290. The production fallback recovered 0.963 +- 0.002 of clean retrieval, while the passing branch recorded a no-harm value of 1.000. CertBind extends multimodal composability from connected representations to certifiable task decisions.

</details>


### [53] [Capacity Confounds and Coverage Guarantees in Adaptive Sub-model Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07157)
*Alireza Moayedikia,Alicia Troncoso Lora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究子模型联邦学习中的自适应容量分配问题，以HAS-FL为测试案例，发现三大结论：更新散度估计的客户端异质性被设备容量主导而非数据；自适应分配存在隐藏失效模式（未覆盖参数停留在随机初始化并逐渐破坏全局模型）；在同等预算下，自适应分配并不优于随机分配甚至更差。子模型训练的价值在于参数覆盖和容量预算，而非分配智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子模型联邦学习方法仅根据设备资源分配模型宽度容量，但已有大量研究建议应根据服务器观测到的客户端更新来估计数据异质性并据此分配容量。作者质疑这一"自然下一步"是否真的可行，希望通过严谨的实验检验其有效性。

Method: 以HAS-FL自适应容量分配框架为测试案例，采用对照实验设计：（1）用修正后的更新散度估计器检验其与真实标签分布散度的相关性，并控制设备容量变量；（2）检验自适应分配在客户端被限制在完整宽度以下的失效模式；（3）设计匹配预算的对照组，比较自适应分配、随机分配和均匀分配三者的性能差异。

Result: （1）更新散度估计的异质性与设备容量强烈负相关，控制容量后无数据信号残留，该混淆此前未被记录；（2）自适应分配存在隐藏失效模式，覆盖保证可消除该问题；（3）匹配预算下，自适应分配在图像基准上与随机分配无差异，在文本基准上甚至最弱且消耗最多容量。

Conclusion: 子模型联邦学习仍有价值，因为它能让资源受限客户端以二次方降低的成本参与训练，但真正保护精度的是参数覆盖而非分配智能。未来设计需要能从容量效应中分离出来的异质性信号。

Abstract: Sub-model federated learning lets resource-constrained clients train width-reduced versions of a global model, but existing methods allocate capacity by device resources alone. A natural next step, allocating capacity by each client's data heterogeneity as estimated from the updates the server already observes, has been repeatedly suggested. We ask whether that step is possible, using HAS-FL, an adaptive capacity-allocation framework, as a test case. Our findings are threefold. First, validated against ground-truth label-distribution divergence on reproducible partitions, update-divergence estimates of client heterogeneity are dominated by capacity rather than data: across two corrected estimators, multiple datasets, and all seeds, the estimates correlate strongly and negatively with device capacity, and no data signal remains once capacity is controlled for. This previously undocumented confound affects any method estimating client statistics from sub-model updates. Second, adaptive allocation has a hidden failure mode: when every client is capped below full width, the uncovered parameters stay at random initialization and progressively corrupt the global model. A simple coverage guarantee removes the failure and explains why uniform allocation collapses. Third, a matched-budget control settles what adaptivity contributes: random allocation to the same average budget performs no differently on both image benchmarks, and on the naturally partitioned text benchmark the adaptive policy is the weakest of the three strategies while consuming the most capacity. Sub-model training remains valuable because it admits constrained clients at quadratically reduced cost, but what protects accuracy is parameter coverage rather than allocation intelligence. Its apparent benefits come from capacity budgeting and coverage, and future designs need heterogeneity signals separable from capacity effects.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在实际部署中面临拜占庭攻击的威胁，攻击者可静默篡改团队计划动作，而学习者只能观测公开信息（计划动作、奖励、状态），无法观测覆盖动作和实际执行动作。现有方法缺乏对这类攻击下安全学习的信息论理解和高效算法，亟需建立理论基础和算法框架。

Method: 首先通过攻击者信息区分两种鲁棒MDP模型：可观察计划动作的攻击者诱导精确的(s,a)-矩形鲁棒MDP（行为凸包由覆盖诱导的公开结果分布构成），盲攻击者诱导s-矩形模型。然后通过构造两个不可区分的单周期实例证明安全遗憾下界Ω(K)。最后提出阶段绑定的鲁棒估计到决策学习器（stage-tied robust estimation-to-decisions learner）并分析其遗憾界。

Result: 证明了安全遗憾可以精确分解为返回遗憾与累积响应差距D_K之和；构造了两个不可区分的单周期实例，强制产生Ω(K)期望安全遗憾（此时返回遗憾为零），证明对D_K的依赖不可避免；所提算法的遗憾界为Õ(H²S√AK)+E[D_K]。

Conclusion: 该研究为拜占庭攻击下多智能体系统的安全性学习提供了全面的理论和算法基础，明确了攻击者信息的几何影响、安全学习的信息论极限以及有效算法。

Abstract: We study online cooperative control of a multi-agent system under Byzantine attacks. Namely, an unknown, fixed subset of agents are Byzantine comprised and can stealthily overwrite its own coordinates of the team's planned joint action after observing that plan. The learner observes planned actions, public rewards, and public states, but neither the overwrite nor the executed joint action. Our objective is security: to optimize the team performance against the worst overwrites and achieve the optimal security value. We first show that the attacker's information determines the geometry. An attacker that observes the planned action induces an exact $(s,a)$-rectangular robust Markov decision process (MDP) whose rows are convex hulls of overwrite-induced public-outcome laws, whereas a blind attacker induces an $s$-rectangular model. We then identify the information-theoretic limit of security learning, showing that the security regret decomposes exactly into return regret against the response generating the data and a cumulative response gap $D_K$. Two indistinguishable horizon-one instances force $Ω(K)$ expected security regret while return regret is zero, showing that dependence on $D_K$ is unavoidable. Finally, we develop a stage-tied robust estimation-to-decisions learner and prove a regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left(H^2S\sqrt{AK}\right)+\mathbb E[D_K]$. Our studies thus provide comprehensive theoretical and algorithmic foundations of reliable multi-agent systems under Byzantine attacks.

</details>
